<h1>Campus Counts </h1>
<p>Campus Counts has all the publicly accessible graphical display of data on enrollment, applications, degrees, and retention/graduation rates.</p>
	<br>
<div class="row">
  <div class="col-5">
    <div class="nav flex-column nav-pills" id="v-pills-tab" role="tablist" aria-orientation="vertical">
      <a class="nav-link active cc" id="v-pills-overview-tab" data-toggle="pill" href="#v-pills-overview" role="tab" aria-controls="v-pills-overview" aria-selected="true">Overview</a>
      <a class="nav-link cc" id="v-pills-admissions-tab" data-toggle="pill" href="#v-pills-admissions" role="tab" aria-controls="v-pills-admissions" aria-selected="true">Admissions</a>
      <a class="nav-link cc" id="v-pills-employees-tab" data-toggle="pill" href="#v-pills-employees" role="tab" aria-controls="v-pills-employees" aria-selected="false">Employees</a>
      <a class="nav-link cc" id="v-pills-enrollments-tab" data-toggle="pill" href="#v-pills-enrollments" role="tab" aria-controls="v-pills-enrollments" aria-selected="false">Enrollments</a>
      <a class="nav-link cc" id="v-pills-retention-tab" data-toggle="pill" href="#v-pills-retention" role="tab" aria-controls="v-pills-retention" aria-selected="false">Retention &amp; Completions</a>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="col-7">
    <div class="tab-content" id="v-pills-tabContent">
      <div class="tab-pane fade show active" id="v-pills-overview" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="v-pills-overview-tab">
        <p>The Campus Overview provides a quick summary of enrollments, degrees, and faculty at the university. While all of these figures are examined in more detail in the PDFs below, the Campus Overview is one-stop-source for our most commonly requested figures.</p>
        <!-- <p><a class="slide-link" href="CampusOverview.pdf" onClick="var that=this;_gaq.push(['_trackEvent','Download','
Campus Overview',this.href]);setTimeout(function(){location.href=that.href;},200);return false;">Campus Overview</a> - <em>updated November 2020</em></p>-->
        <p><a class="slide-link" href="CampusOverview.pdf" onClick="var that=this;_gaq.push(['_trackEvent','CampusCounts','Overview',this.href]);setTimeout(function(){location.href=that.href;},200);return false;">Campus Overview</a> - <em>updated November 2025</em></p>
      </div>
      <div class="tab-pane fade" id="v-pills-admissions" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="v-pills-admissions-tab">
        <p>Admissions data include numbers of applications, acceptances, and enrollments for three years, as well as students' high-school or college GPAs, and SAT scores for undergraduate applications. Undergraduate student data looks at students who enter as new freshman or new transfer students; graduate data focuses on student admissions by program level. Admissions reports were <em> updated in November 2025.</em></p>
        <p><a class="slide-link" href="Admissions/apps_ug.pdf">Undergraduate Admissions Summary</a></p>
        <p><a class="slide-link" href="Admissions/apps_grad.pdf" onclick="var that=this;_gaq.push(['_trackEvent','Download','Apps',this.href]);setTimeout(function(){location.href=that.href;},200);return false;">Graduate Admissions Summary</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="tab-pane fade" id="v-pills-employees" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="v-pills-employees-tab">
        <p>University employees are made up of faculty, staff, and graduate assistants. In this section you will find information on race/ethnicity, gender, and full-time/part-time status.
          <!--Employee reports were <em>updated in November 2024.</em>-->
        </p>
        <!--<p><a class="slide-link" href="Employees/employeesumm.pdf" onclick="var that=this;_gaq.push(['_trackEvent','Download','Employees',this.href]);setTimeout(function(){location.href=that.href;},200);return false;">Fall 2021 Employees &amp; Graduate Assistants</a></p>-->
        <p><a class="slide-link" href="Employees/employeesumm.pdf" onclick="var that=this;_gaq.push(['_trackEvent','Download','Employees',this.href]);setTimeout(function(){location.href=that.href;},200);return false;">Fall 2025 Employees &amp; Graduate Assistants</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="tab-pane fade" id="v-pills-enrollments" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="v-pills-enrollments-tab">
        <p>Student profiles provide a three-year snapshot of enrollment activity at the University. In these reports you will find data on race/ethnicity, gender, class standing, residency, full-time/part-time status, and more. The figures presented are the official enrollment figures for the University (those reported to various state and federal agencies, as well as those used internally by the University for most purposes). Additional enrollment reports are available that detail information such as where UMD students come from and enrollment by college. Unless otherwise noted, Enrollment reports were <em>updated in November 2025.</em></p>
        <h4>Undergraduate Student Profiles</h4>
          <ul class="current_reports"><li><a class="slide-link" href="Enrollments/stuprofile_allug.pdf" onclick="var that=this;_gaq.push(['_trackEvent','Download','Enroll Profile',this.href]);setTimeout(function(){location.href=that.href;},200);return false;">All Undergraduates</a></li>

          </ul>

      </div>
      <div class="tab-pane fade" id="v-pills-retention" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="v-pills-retention-tab">
        <p>Included in this section are reports on degrees granted for the past 10 years at the University at all levels. Undergraduate retention and graduation rates are split providing rates for students who began at the University as new freshmen or as new transfer students. Unless noted otherwise, Retention and Completion reports were <em>updated in September 2026.</em></p>
        <h4>Retention/Graduation Rates</h4>
        <ul class="current_reports">

  <p slot="eyebrow">    Academics
  </p>
              
  
  
  <h1  slot="headline">
    A World-Sized Classroom
  </h1>

              
  
  
  <div  slot="text">
    <p>Maryland offers more than 300 degree-granting programs, many of them ranked among the nation’s best. </p><p>Terps embrace opportunities beyond campus in D.C., across Maryland and around the globe to expand their learning and perspectives. </p>
  </div>

      <div slot="text">
    <p>With over 100 majors across 12 schools and colleges, we have a major to match your interests or can help you design your own.</p>

  <div >
    
                        
                    
      
      
      
      <h2  class="mb-md umd-sans-largest-uppercase">
        Top of the Class
      </h2>
    
                        
      
      <div  class="umd-text-rich-advanced mb-sm">
        <p>Terps are high achievers, knowledge seekers and creative leaders. They earn national recognition, win prestigious competitions and shine on all kinds of stages. </p>
      </div>
    
          
      
      <div  class="umd-layout-grid-inline-tablet-rows">

              </div>

        <p slot="headline">
    Academic Support
  </p>

        <div slot="text">
    <p>From advising to tutoring to career planning, UMD is here with an array of resources to help Terps succeed in their educational and personal journeys.</p>
  </div>

        <p slot="eyebrow">
    Find your people
  </p>

        <p slot="headline">
    Living-Learning and Other Special Programs
  </p>

        <div slot="text">
    <p>Maryland’s highly regarded programs bring together students with a common interest to explore their passions, make connections with one another and faculty members, and participate in unique opportunities designed to enhance their education. More than half of all first-year Terps participate in one of more than two dozen special programs—most of them residential.</p>
  </div>

    <section

                  <div class="umd-layout-space-horizontal-normal">
          <div class="umd-layout-grid-masonry umd-animation-grid">

      <div slot="text">
    <p>UMD has 9 million titles in 7 libraries and online—and so much more than books, including a variety of study spaces and tech to support learning.</p>
  </div>

    

      
  <p slot="headline">

    </umd-element-card-overlay>

      </a>

  
    
      </p>

      </a>

      <div slot="text">
    <p>Undecided on a major? Letters &amp; Sciences offers programs and services to help Terps transition to UMD and determine their academic path. </p>

    <div class="max-bound rhs-page-body">
    <div class="outer-pad">
      <div  data-history-node-id="38621" about="/programs/undergraduate/academics/academic-majors">
        
        <div class="editor-content">
          <h1>Academic Majors</h1>
                                <p>Undergraduates at Smith learn from world-class faculty who deliver a state-of-the-art curriculum based on the needs of the real world. With our&nbsp;<a href="https://www.rhsmith.umd.edu/programs/plus-1">Plus 1 program</a>, students have the option to earn their bachelor's and master's degrees in just five years for select majors.</p>
                  </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
    <div class="page-section page-section-umd_smith_page page-section-us_wrapper">
    <div class="max-bound">
      <div class="outer-pad">
          <div class="rhs-wrapper bg-umd-lighter-gray">
              <div class="page-section page-section-us_wrapper page-section-ut_text">
    <div class="inner-pad">
      
	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11036">
				<h2><a id="accounting"></a>Accounting</h2><p>When you study Accounting at Smith, you’ll learn from faculty who influence&nbsp;<a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=cd8dT0FuqQ4&amp;feature=emb_title">how companies choose to invest in cybersecurity</a>, and network with Smith accounting alumni, who hold more CEO and managing partner positions in public accounting firms than any other university.</p><p>The accounting curriculum includes financial planning, budgeting, accounting systems, financial management controls, financial analysis of performance, financial reporting, internal and external auditing, and taxation. It provides an excellent foundation for careers like public accounting, management, forensic accounting, and consulting.</p>
			</div>
				
	</div>

    </div>
  </div>
    <div class="page-section page-section-us_wrapper page-section-ut_accordion">
    <div class="inner-pad">
      

	<div class="accordion" id="accordion-11046">
																																																																																																									<div class="card">
										<div class="card-header" id="accordion-11046-header-16">
												<button class="btn" data-toggle="collapse" data-target="#accordion-11046-content-16" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="accordion-11046-content-16">
							Degree Requirements / 4-Year Plan
							<br>
							<span class="accordion-item-summary"></span>
						</button>
					</div>
					<div id="accordion-11046-content-16" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="accordion-11046-header-16" >
						<div class="card-body">
							                
	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11206">
				<h3>Download complete degree requirements and/or a 4-year plan.</h3><p><strong>New Requirements</strong></p><p><strong>Public Accounting (CPA) Track:</strong> Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2024 and later. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2024 have the option of adopting these new requirements. The Public Accounting Track is for students who are pursuing careers that require (or recommend) that they sit for the CPA (Certified Public Accounting) exam. Also, Accounting majors who want to keep the CPA career track open as an option should target the Public Accounting track. Please consult your Smith School Academic Advisor if you have questions.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="e32f8b2cd0bbe629520482519c3b41415"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1jfNXQkyh1MekPbTe0ObZ_ia-_QgTCu3a/view?usp=drive_link" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a></li><li data-list-item-id="ec9fba62ff2dc343c9f74ad84ed193e42"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1v8e3iCQbtn6j90NkhcQaLFNfkuG7uIaw/view?usp=drive_link" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a></li></ul><p><strong>Business and Accounting Advisory Track:</strong> Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2024 and later. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2024 have the option of adopting these new requirements. The Business and Accounting Advisory Track is for students who are pursuing careers in business and accounting that do not require the student to be CPA Exam eligible. Please consult your Smith School Academic Advisor if you have questions.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="ef6a96ae561793d4e38d215c867305f6b"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1lul_RQfaaeX0G0FE2ROooAVc84KqcbN_/view?usp=drive_link" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a></li><li data-list-item-id="e36ed6d8b739e85211cf8599c078a582b"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1sxnwZal5e80gvW6AjavevxGAFvDS1Y_N/view?usp=drive_link" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a></li></ul><p><strong>Old Requirements</strong></p><p>- (New General Education) Effective for students entering UMD in Spring 2014 and later. Students who entered UMD prior to Spring 2014 have the option of adopting these new requirements. Please consult your Smith School Academic Advisor if you have questions.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="ea049980966872795f99e3acc1ee4c54c"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1kOUMaj51Nah2iW_OG3DZYjQju7GWiFhD/view?usp=share_link" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a> [ PDF]</li><li data-list-item-id="e80df2ae1926269337f34f9c72d8f7978"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1shdiNV56ddEspwQZPN9wZdEKP3sh2uEx/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a> [ PDF ]</li></ul><p>- (New General Education) Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2012 through Fall 2013. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2012 have the option of adopting these requirements. External transfer students will be required to complete the new Smith School core requirements (launched in 2011) beginning Fall 2013. Please consult your Smith School Academic Advisor if you have questions.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="e0decbb49d5fd99c4c442f93f862259f2"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1om79sYMqna_UU8_I2-dCi4VkDRYmys1T/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a> [ PDF ]</li><li data-list-item-id="e0d2b92ae0529766829ac5da891a9a2f8"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1x9v5BFWaMzxnxCjyxX62cPYVWofWuHjv/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a> [ PDF ]</li></ul><p>- (CORE General Education) Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2011 through Spring 2012. External transfer students will be required to complete the new Smith School core requirements (launched in 2011) beginning Fall 2013.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="e11c4291a2246d2cad76b91a6a10dcd56"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1X4Can9bpF9NVokn8FUFKqZnYXDcsaM_u/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a> [ PDF ]</li><li data-list-item-id="e6817dceac5babfd884d6b660ce596f54"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1H9ZgWY-2TJqpXwNayMM246BW5yh94biV/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a> [ PDF ]</li></ul><p>- (CORE General Education) Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2007 through Spring 2011.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="ecad070be06f2f93e82fe2e9b3751998c"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1bjwFjslSmVp-G1GEpgQKkIn2pVXGFjcF/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a> [ PDF ]</li><li data-list-item-id="eba349c41c6c3bca967ff431e36a65a26"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1UhEm-yc0udrUv8tZtN5BskMc45HzEtW8/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a> [ PDF ]</li></ul><p>- (CORE General Education) Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2005 through Spring 2007.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="e984a0f8354750c486ebe465b6b2d03bc"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/11JqHRwhuPNgdsxkF2LdsjPEhrjEiVjHD/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Freshman/Sophomore Requirements</a> [ PDF ]</li><li data-list-item-id="e0271cbbcb94794bcf54e2d90d8bb0576"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1LMBzN7NYzcOxW8U75a3oH8qA0Yevka4t/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Junior/Senior Requirements</a> [ PDF ]</li><li data-list-item-id="ec03e323a9c7f9f33c59f226074d09566"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1vZrk9kqqJRs48p-Ig_r_q93EGMdjKRAL/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a> [ credits</strong></p></td></tr></tbody></table><h3>Suggested 150 Credit Hour Options</h3><p>Most states, including Maryland, require a minimum of 150 credit hours of college education to fulfill the education requirements to obtain a CPA license. Students can typically complete their Accounting degree and the specific course requirements for the CPA in the minimum 120 credits required to earn the degree. However, if 150 credits are required to fulfill the education requirements to obtain a CPA license, students must complete 30 credits beyond the specific course requirements to be licensed. These additional credits can be completed in several ways, as illustrated below. Please consult the Board of Accountants in the state where you intend to take the exam for their specific requirements because requirements can vary between states. For details on various states' CPA requirements visit <a href="http://www.nasba.org/" target="_blank">www.nasba.org</a>.</p><p><strong>Graduate Programs</strong></p><p>The Smith School offers several specialty master's degrees including one in accounting. Credits from these programs can apply to the 150 credits required for the CPA, and these programs can further enhance your accounting knowledge and skills or provide you with more depth in another area of business. In addition, accounting majors can pursue +1 Dual Bachelor's/Master's degrees and take up to 10 credits of graduate credit as an undergraduate at undergraduate tuition rates. In addition, some graduate credits can satisfy undergraduate degree requirements. For more details on these programs visit <a href="/programs/business-masters">Business Master’s</a> and <a href="/programs/plus-1">Plus 1</a>.</p><p>In addition, many institutions offer graduate programs for undergraduate accounting students. Consult institutions in your area about the availability of these types of graduate degrees.</p><p><strong>Double Majors/Degrees</strong></p><p>Accounting majors can select a second major in or outside the Smith School. Any credits required to complete the second major, above the 120 minimum required for a degree, can be applied toward the 150 total credits to obtain a CPA license. Students who accumulate 150 credits while completing both majors are eligible to earn two degrees upon their graduation.</p><p>Obtain information on <a href="https://academiccatalog.umd.edu/undergraduate/programs/" target="_blank">majors outside the Smith School</a>.</p><p><strong>Minors</strong></p><p>The Smith School of Business currently <a href="/programs/undergraduate/academics/academic-minors">offers two minors</a> that are open to Smith School students, Business Analytics and Innovation &amp; Entrepreneurship. Additionally, <a href="https://www.admissions.umd.edu/explore/colleges-and-schools/majors/minors" target="_blank">many minors are offered in other departments across the campus</a>.</p><p><strong>Smith Undergraduate Fellows Program</strong></p><p>The <a href="https://www.rhsmith.umd.edu/programs/undergraduate/academics/fellows-programs">Smith Undergraduate Fellows program</a> is an innovative concept in undergraduate business education, offering a series of special academic programs—or tracks—that will create small communities of scholars within the larger Smith School community. Each specialized fellows program combines opportunities for action learning and professional development with rigorous in-depth academic coursework focused on cutting-edge issues affecting 21st-century business.</p><p><strong>Study Abroad Programs (summer, winter, and/or semester programs)</strong></p><p>Studying abroad is a great way to expand your understanding of other business and cultural environments, and to demonstrate an ability to work effectively in foreign environments. In addition to the campus <a href="https://marylandglobal.umd.edu/global-learning-all/study-abroad" target="_blank">Study Abroad Office</a>, the <a href="/centers-initiatives/global-business">Center for Global Business</a> offers exchange programs for Smith School students. Visit the links below for more details on all your program options and contact information.</p>

	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11056">
				<h2><a id="finance"></a>Finance</h2><p>Smith’s Finance majors learn from faculty with real-world experience, like <a href="/smith-profiles/journey-begun-africa-never-along-single-path">Professor Lemma Senbet</a>, whose international finance career has influenced policy in Africa and the careers of the students he’s mentored, who include a bank president and a chief economist at the SEC.&nbsp;</p><p>The finance curriculum will familiarize you with the institutions, theory and practice involved in the allocation of financial resources within the private sector. It provides an excellent foundation for careers involving financial analysis and management, investment analysis and portfolio management, investment banking, insurance and risk management, banking, and international finance.</p><p>While only MATH120 Elementary Calculus I or MATH140 Calculus I (MATH130 also accepted) is required for the finance major, students with a strong math background are encouraged to complete both MATH140 Calculus I and MATH141 Calculus II. In addition, these students are encouraged to complete MATH240 Introduction to Linear Algebra because the material in this course provides a deeper understanding of finance. Students with an advanced math background will be in a better position to compete for admission to the Quantitative Finance Fellows and the Wall Street Fellows programs. In addition, this quantitative background will expand a student's career opportunities within finance.</p>
			</div>
				
	</div>

	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11076">
				<h2><a id="information-systems"></a>Information Systems</h2><p>The Smith School’s Information Systems (IS) major is ranked No. 6 in the country (and the school is No. 11 among publics) by U.S. News &amp; World Report and includes stellar faculty with real-world experience and influential research, like Louiqa Raschid, who is <a href="/news/raschid-receives-nsf-funding-improve-supply-chain-knowledge-networks-covid-19-medical-supplies">optimizing supply chain networks</a> for desperately needed medical equipment during the pandemic.</p><p>The IS major prepares you to effectively plan, use and manage information technologies and systems through a focus on system design and implementation skills, including database and web design. These core concepts provide an excellent foundation for careers in consulting, data analytics and information systems management.</p><p>View&nbsp;<a href="https://go.umd.edu/ishub-questions">common IS major questions</a>&nbsp;in the&nbsp;<a href="https://go.umd.edu/ishub">Smith Information Systems Hub</a>.</p>

					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11096">
				<h2><a id="international-business"></a>International Business</h2><p>The International Business major dynamically pairs your interests in international economics systems and their inherent multicultural characteristics. In addition to the usual core course and electives that comprise the degree, you’ll have the opportunity to select an upper-level foreign language course to truly expand your skills in a global capacity.</p>

	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11116">
				<h2><a id="management"></a>Management</h2><p>When you select a Management major, you join a leading undergraduate program in management and entrepreneurship,&nbsp;<a href="/smith-profiles/edu-preneur-sparks-innovation-smith">led by “edu-preneurs” like Oliver Schlake</a>. Both of these programs are regularly highly ranked, and focus on leadership and entrepreneurship, engaging students in critical thinking and problem solving, which are applicable to many careers.</p><p>With a management major you have a wealth of potential career paths. These can include consulting analyst, financial advisor, human resource management, nonprofit management, and entrepreneurship, including social entrepreneurship.</p>
			</div>
				
	</div>

					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11136">
				<h2><a id="marketing"></a>Marketing</h2><p>As a Smith marketing major, you’ll learn how to be successful at all levels of an organization by ensuring that quality goods and services are provided at fair prices and in a way that benefits the community and society. And you’ll learn from faculty like Rebecca Ratner, whose <a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uNAnHvHCUzs&amp;feature=youtu.be">research about solitude</a> has made a huge impact in consumer studies.</p><p>The marketing major’s curriculum lays an excellent groundwork for careers in brand management, advertising, consumer research, and sales.</p>

					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11156">
				<h2><a id="operations-management"></a>Operations Management &amp; Business Analytics</h2><p>The Operations Management &amp; Business Analytics major will equip you with the knowledge and skills to apply quantitative and statistically based modeling techniques to data. And with world-class faculty at the helm of your education, you’ll be able to drive and influence decision-making at all levels of business. These skills are in higher demand than ever before, and provide career opportunities in the public and private sectors, including careers in energy, finance, insurance, health care, logistics, and marketing.</p>

External transfer students will be required to complete the new Smith School core requirements (launched in 2011) beginning Fall 2013.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="e9a3d09a86efb4189c5b4450236125b00"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1FAARETJ3Kkdg2zEcvWgcDHlSyKavCJbc/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a>&nbsp;[PDF]</li><li data-list-item-id="e9350249cba52ce50d1a2efd355e6d1f2"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1XD6YcRsZJFCaGyAWRuD46cb5CUiNdKLZ/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a>&nbsp;[PDF]</li></ul><p>(CORE General Education) Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2005 through Spring 2011.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="e93ec721344053195812223d3e6c4c64e"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1DuwYSYvlnHi6f0b2E8W4k9WShkPHq22O/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a>&nbsp;[PDF]</li><li data-list-item-id="edf6b839d993f1129100c83402b8c97a8"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1SXMKMfHNApHcLIppeNwWmGI7TStgaT9H/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a>&nbsp;[PDF]</li></ul><p>(CORE General Education) Effective for students entering UMD from Fall 1996 through Spring 2005</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="eade5c5c39503ec2a1178099ed1dee508"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1Y2v7KJHWWe5NwZ_Nx4DZZpizcmXQGpl6/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Freshmen/Sophomore Requirements</a>&nbsp;[PDF]</li><li data-list-item-id="e1f1786757f02a18f13cb00c86b282c40"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1J9SMfQLbeiKYVMr9F1jC0s3t4KU7mj_7/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Junior/Senior Requirements</a>&nbsp;[PDF]</li><li data-list-item-id="e2f611b9bf17c9d71d24706708c26250a"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1AM-zfD-lDlpv7UsUvXWiGgHPYIubGvUZ/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan - Technical Track</a>&nbsp;[PDF]</li><li data-list-item-id="ea16620b92229c5cd9764a55c5df83617"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/13Wu2GFbZUOVsPDXgJHIzigGcE3DkLg_7/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan - Managerial Track</a>&nbsp;[PDF]</li></ul><p><strong>Links to Related Sites</strong></p><ul><li data-list-item-id="e939dbb014bc0024d7b2bd747d4fa9a0d"><a href="https://academiccatalog.umd.edu/undergraduate/approved-courses/bmgt/" target="_blank">Course Descriptions and Prerequisite Information</a></li><li data-list-item-id="e35a16256cbe97aebaf5e218ce5f31e38"><a href="/faculty-research/academic-departments/decision-operations-information-technologies">Decision, Operations &amp; Information Technologies Department</a></li><li data-list-item-id="ebf19e25f09fbb9ea42afd06365d79316">Additional Operations Management &amp; Business Analytics major and career information</li></ul><h2>Major Requirements:</h2><table class="table bg-umd-white"><tbody><tr><td>BMGT332 - Quantitative Models for Management Decisions</td><td style="width:150px;"><p class="text-align-right">3 credits</p></td></tr><tr><td>BMGT385 - Operations Management</td><td><p class="text-align-right">3 credits</p></td></tr><tr><td>BMGT430 - Data Modeling in Business</td><td><p class="text-align-right">3 credits</p></td></tr><tr><td>BMGT431 - Data Analytics and AI for Business</td><td><p class="text-align-right">3 credits</p></td></tr><tr><td><p>Two of the following courses from List A or 1 course from List A and 1 course from List B:</p><p>List A:<br>BMGT434 – Analytics Consulting: Cases and Projects, or BMGT490H QUEST Capstone Professional Practicum<br>BMGT435 – Business Process Simulation</p><p>List B:<br>BMGT400 – Storytelling with AI and Data Visualization (formerly BMGT408V)<br>BMGT401 – Big Data and AI Infrastructure (formerly BMGT408E)<br>BMGT404 - Essential Data and AI Skills for Business Analytics (CMSC/DATA320 Introduction to Data Science can substitute)<br>BMGT485 - Project Management (INST453 Project Management for Information Science can substitute)</p></td><td><p class="text-align-right">6 credits</p></td></tr><tr><td><strong>Total</strong></td><td><p class="text-align-right"><strong>18 credits</strong></p></td></tr></tbody></table>

    <div class="page-section page-section-umd_smith_page page-section-us_wrapper">
    <div class="max-bound">
      <div class="outer-pad">
          <div class="rhs-wrapper ">
              <div class="page-section page-section-us_wrapper page-section-ut_text">
    <div class="inner-pad">
      
	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11176">
				<h2><a id="supply-chain"></a>Supply Chain Management</h2><p>Smith Supply Chain Management majors learn what it is to be the lynchpin holding an entire organization together. You’ll learn from renowned faculty with industry experience,&nbsp;<a href="/smith-profiles/leaving-industry-and-finding-something-more">like Adams Steven</a>, to ensure that the desired product is available to the customer in the right condition and quantity and at the right time, place, and cost. The curriculum incorporates state-of-the-art software applications, so you’ll learn to develop ways of increasing the efficiency of the flow of goods and services across organizations.</p><p>Supply Chain Management majors often double major with complementary majors like Operations Management, Marketing, Information Systems, International Business, and Finance.</p>
			</div>
				
	</div>

    </div>
  </div>
    <div class="page-section page-section-us_wrapper page-section-ut_accordion">
    <div class="inner-pad">
      

	<div class="accordion" id="accordion-11186">

							Degree Requirements / 4-Year Plan
							<br>
							<span class="accordion-item-summary"></span>
						</button>
					</div>
					<div id="accordion-11186-content-16" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="accordion-11186-header-16" >
						<div class="card-body">
							                
	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11426">
				<h3>Download complete degree requirements and/or a 4-year plan</h3><p><strong>New Requirements</strong></p><p>Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2024 and later. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2024 have the option of adopting these new requirements. Please consult your Smith School Academic Advisor if you have questions.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="e03cfe0e135521e2ba3cae1e7722f1a77"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1RV75U2inTI46qxmT3iQq-PFkCWZwT6ts/view?usp=drive_link" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a>&nbsp;[PDF]</li><li data-list-item-id="eba59665c750c6c291ea6fd4b70e1d8bc"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1EL77uJRpzjvyhmxDXJIfRuSCtQF3e5pF/view?usp=drive_link" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a>&nbsp;[PDF]</li></ul><p><strong>Old Requirements</strong></p><p>(New General Education) Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2020 and later. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2020 have the option of adopting these new requirements. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2016 have the option of adopting these new requirements. Introduction to Transportation</td><td style="width:150px;"><p class="text-align-right">3 credits</p></td></tr><tr><td>BMGT372 - Introduction to Logistics Management and Supply Chain Management</td><td><p class="text-align-right">3 credits</p></td></tr><tr><td>Three of the following courses:&nbsp;<br>BMGT373 - Purchasing and Inbound Logistics&nbsp;<br>BMGT475 - Supply Chain Strategy and Network Design&nbsp;<br>BMGT476 - Technology Applications in Supply Chain Management&nbsp;<br>BMGT477 - International Supply Chain Management&nbsp;<br>BMGT478 -
